## Review

**7 out of 10, Effective.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck has a clear route from overview and thesis through capabilities, industry dynamics and financials to a conclusion, and its visual system is consistent: navy ground, orange emphasis, light panels, and a footer on every page. Slides 4, 14 and 28 show strong hierarchy, readable key numbers and clean charts, and the bridges on slide 28 make the 2025 and 2026 story easy to follow. The main weaknesses are density and legibility on several slides: footnotes are tiny (4, 14, 28), the map slides (9, 11) have small bullet text next to big stats, and the disclaimer (2) and presenter bios (27) are walls of text. Slide 28 repeats nearly identical bridges with $0.0 labels and vague bar scaling, which weakens the chart, and the deck reads better as a read-ahead than as a projected talk.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the navy and orange identity, the large orange or blue key figures with short labels (slides 4, 6, 10), and the paired bridge structure on slide 28.

**Most useful repair:** Enlarge or cut the body text and footnotes on slides 9, 11 and 14, and fix the 2026 bridge on slide 28 so the small $0.0 steps and bar proportions read clearly.

**What would change the score:** Replace the several panel-and-bullet slides with fewer, more visual pages that carry one takeaway each, and move the bios and disclaimer text into smaller appendix material.

_Confidence medium. 32 of 32 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck opens with a title slide naming CEO Joe Liotine and CFO Doug Ostermann, followed by forward-looking statement and non-GAAP disclaimers. It then moves into a business overview with key metrics on revenue, end markets and regions, and a one-slide investment thesis. The middle sections cover the company's electrical architecture product portfolio, its proprietary engineering tool suite, the engineering and manufacturing model, and the supply chain. Next come industry dynamics, including secular trends and content-per-vehicle growth, and a financials overview with a 2026 to 2028 framework and 2026 guidance. The closing slide summarizes the value-creation case, and the appendix holds presenter bios and non-GAAP reconciliations. The deck is built around dense data callouts with footnoted sourcing, and it uses a consistent footer on every page. Several slides use a few large figures with short supporting bullets, which makes the key numbers easy to scan. The financial slides pair a revenue bridge and an EBITDA bridge, each showing the 2025 starting point and the 2026 guidance.

## Questions about this deck

**How many slides does the Versigent investor presentation have?**

The Versigent investor presentation has 32 pages. Slide 1 is the cover and names CEO Joe Liotine and CFO Doug Ostermann, and the appendix holds presenter bios and non-GAAP reconciliations.

**What revenue outlook does the Versigent deck show through 2028?**

Slide 21 shows revenue of $8.8B in 2025, with a 3% adjusted CAGR and a projected 3-4% adjusted CAGR from 2025 to 2028E, reaching ~$10B in 2028E. Slide 20 summarizes the framework as a revenue CAGR of 3-4% through 2028E.

**What does Versigent say about its engineering tools and engineering team?**

Slide 9 states that proprietary engineering tools allow for a 50% reduction in drawing time for electrical architectures. The same slide lists ~8K highly skilled engineers, 875 engineers working hand-in-hand with customers, and ~700 patents and patent applications as of December 31, 2025.

**What does the Versigent deck say about its supply chain?**

Slide 10 reports 95% supplier visibility to at least Tier 3 level and that more than 95% of cable is sourced in-region. It also states that order-to-VIN specific delivery takes less than 10 days.

Non-GAAP Financial Measures
This presentation contains information about the Company’s financial results which are not presented in accordance with GAAP, including adjusted revenue, adjusted EBITDA, adjusted EBITDA margin, free cash flow and free cash flow conversion. Management believes the non-GAAP financial measures used in this presentation are useful to both management and investors in their analysis of the Company’s financial position, results of operations and liquidity. In particular, management believes that each of adjusted revenue, adjusted EBITDA, adjusted EBITDA margin, free cash flow and free cash flow conversion is a useful measure in assessing the Company’s ongoing financial performance that, when reconciled to the corresponding GAAP measure, provides improved comparability between periods through the exclusion of certain items that management believes are not indicative of the Company’s core operating performance and that may obscure underlying business results and trends. Management also uses certain of these non-GAAP financial measures for internal planning and forecasting purposes. Such non-GAAP financial measures are reconciled to the most directly comparable GAAP financial measures in the attached supplemental schedules in the appendix. Non-GAAP measures should not be considered in isolation or as a substitute for our reported results prepared in accordance with GAAP and, as calculated, may not be comparable to other similarly titled measures of other companies.

Investor Presentation | March 2026 | Versigent

**Layout**

Single-column title slide with the title block left-aligned at roughly the vertical middle of the slide, occupying the left third; a decorative ribbon graphic fills the right half and rises from the top edge, sweeping down to the lower right. A footer row sits along the bottom with the page number at the left margin, the footer text beside it, and the Versigent logo at the bottom-right corner.

**Visuals**

Decorative illustration of about eight to ten parallel golden ribbons (gradient from light gold #E0A93B to darker amber #C07F2A) that enter from the top edge around x 50-88%, curve down and to the left in an S-shape, then fan out and terminate in angled cut ends toward the lower right around x 70-97%, y 75-86%. The ribbons have subtle shading suggesting a three-dimensional twisted band. Illustration is not cropped at the top edge; it bleeds off the top. Logo: Versigent logo at bottom right consisting of an orange-amber (#D9742B) abstract mark of two curved leaf-like strokes in a circle, followed by the white wordmark 'Versigent' in a sans-serif typeface.

**Design**

Background: Solid navy #16283F, covering the full slide. Dark navy background #16283F with no gradient or texture. ### Slide 4

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A dense single-page metrics grid pairs large headline numbers with a small donut-style breakdown for end markets and regions, so the scale of the business reads at a glance.
- Review: Slides 4, 14 and 28 show strong hierarchy, readable key numbers and clean charts, and the bridges on slide 28 make the 2025 and 2026 story easy to follow.
- Worth keeping: Keep the navy and orange identity, the large orange or blue key figures with short labels (slides 4, 6, 10), and the paired bridge structure on slide 28.

**Layout**

Single-slide title block at top left, left-aligned at about 3 percent from the left edge, with the title baseline near 17 percent of the height. Below it two equal rounded-free rectangular panels sit side by side on a dark navy field, left panel Key Metrics (x 3.6 to 49.5 percent) and right panel 2025 Revenue (x 51.2 to 97.1 percent), both spanning y 24.6 to 83.9 percent. Footnotes run in four lines across the bottom in grey, with the slide number 4 at the lower left, the footer text beside it, and the Versigent logo at the lower right.

**Visuals**

Left panel: six rows, each with a line icon at left, a black label and a bold blue value at right, separated by five grey dotted horizontal rules. Icons in order: an orange rising bar chart with an upward arrow (2025 Revenue); a blue hand holding an orange coin with a dollar sign (EBITDA margin); a blue globe outline with orange continents (Employees); an orange globe with a network of blue nodes (Production revenue with OEMs); blue people silhouettes with an orange star (vehicle platforms); a blue outline of a control unit with an orange block network (E/E architectures).
Right panel: two donut charts with no axes, no legend box, and no title other than the headings Regions and End Markets. Regions donut, segments clockwise from the top: North America 40 percent in #0857C3 (top and right upper arc), Europe, Middle East and Africa 24 percent in light cyan #74D2E7 (right lower arc), Asia Pacific 33 percent in dark green #004624 (lower left arc), Rest of World 3 percent in dark navy #16283F (small wedge at upper left). Each segment is separated by a thin white gap. End Markets donut, clockwise from the top: Automotive 90 percent in #0857C3 (large arc), Commercial Vehicle 10 percent in #74D2E7 (upper left arc), Other Industrials under 1 percent in dark green #004624 (thin sliver at the top). Labels sit outside each ring with their percentages in bold beneath the names. No values are estimated; all are printed. No highlighted points.

**Design**

Background: Solid dark navy #16283F full-bleed background. Dark navy full-bleed background with light blue-grey panels. Title is white Calibri Bold, large, about 7.4 percent of slide height for cap height block, sentence case. Section headings Key Metrics and 2025 Revenue | $8.8B are bold orange #CD7925. Chart subheadings Regions and End Markets are bold blue #0857C3. Metric values are bold blue #0857C3 at about 3.7 percent of slide height. Labels are black Calibri regular at about 2.6 percent. Chart labels are black Calibri at about 2.2 percent, with percentages in Calibri Bold. Footnotes are grey #7F7F7F Calibri at about 1.8 percent; footer text is grey Calibri at about 2.0 percent. Panels have square corners and no shadow. Dotted separators are grey. Accents are blue #0857C3 and orange #CD7925.

### Slide 5

**Text**

Versigent Investment Thesis
Secular trends driving demands for higher speed, data, and power distribution across vehicles
Differentiated as a full-service solutions provider for OEMs across comprehensive product portfolio
Industry-leading scale in automotive with rigorous and best-in-class operating model
Opportunity for product and end market expansion
Resulting in attractive financial profile and opportunity for accretive capital deployment
5
Investor Presentation | March 2026 | Versigent

**Layout**

Single column of five rows below a left-aligned title at x 3.1%, y 9.5%. Each row pairs a light circular icon at x 8.7% with a left-aligned sentence starting at x 16.5%, with rows spaced about 13.6% of slide height apart from y 27.1% to y 82.7%. A thin orange bracket on the left at about x 5.9% links the five icons. Footer line sits at y 94.3% with the page number at x 3.9% and the footer text at x 7.6%; the Versigent logo sits bottom right at x 85.6%.

**Visuals**

Five identical light-blue circles in a vertical column, each holding a single-colour line icon. Icon 1 (top): blue rising trend line with a dot-circle and an orange tag shape at lower right, suggesting search and growth. Icon 2: stack of three blue chevron-outlined layers with an orange top layer. Icon 3: blue gear with orange arrows pointing outward in all eight directions. Icon 4: orange circular arrows around a blue gear containing a blue checkmark. Icon 5: blue upward arrow rising across orange vertical bars of increasing height. A thin orange bracket line with small orange round dots at each icon's left edge connects the five circles. The Versigent logo at bottom right: orange stylised V-shaped mark with white wordmark 'Versigent'.

**Design**

Background: Solid dark navy #16283F, no pattern or photograph. Dark navy background #16283F with no gradient or texture. **Layout**

Full-bleed dark navy slide with a single-line title across the top left at about 9.5% from the top and a 3.1% left margin. Two equal-height columns sit below: a left Product Portfolio panel (x 3.1 to 46.6%) with two stacked boxes, and a right Key Stats panel (x 53.4 to 96.9%) holding a 2 by 3 grid of stat tiles, with a gradient chevron between them at x 48.5%. The footnote block sits at the bottom left above a footer line with the page number at the far left and the Versigent logo at bottom right.

**Visuals**

A gradient chevron arrow pointing right, centred vertically between the two columns at x 48.5% to 51.4%, y 43.7% to 71.3%; it fades from dark navy at its left edge to orange #CD7925 at its point, with no outline. Six light-blue stat tiles in a 2 by 3 grid with no icons, charts or photographs; the numbers 1, 6, 1, 3, 550, 9, 10 and >50 and >1,900 are set in bold orange. Versigent logo at bottom right: an orange swirl icon followed by the white wordmark Versigent. No photographs, charts or tables.

**Design**

Background solid dark navy #16283F with no gradient or texture. Title white #FFFFFF Calibri Bold, approximately 7.4% of slide height, sans-serif. Panel header bars: Product Portfolio on dark blue #042C61 and Key Stats on orange #CD7925, both with white Calibri Bold text about 3.3% of slide height. Panel bodies and stat tiles are light blue #DFE9F4 with no borders or shadows and square corners. Section headings are dark navy #16283F Calibri Bold about 3.0% of slide height. Body text is black #000000 Calibri regular about 2.6% to 3.0% of slide height. Bullets are small orange #CD7925 round dots. Accent colour is orange #CD7925. Footnotes are grey #7F7F7F Calibri about 1.8% of slide height. Page number and footer are grey #7F7F7F Calibri about 2.0%.

Investor Presentation | March 2026 | Versigent

**Layout**

Three equal columns of light cards sit side by side across the lower-middle of a dark slide, each headed by a numbered node on a horizontal orange timeline. The title sits top-left at the left margin (about 3% from the left edge) above a subtitle line; a footnote sits bottom-left, and the footer (page number, text and logo) runs along the bottom edge, with the Versigent logo bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Three line icons in orange outline on the cards: (1) a car chassis or platform outline with internal architecture blocks, inside a rounded pill-shaped outline, at about x 4.2 y 47.1 w 6.7 h 6.7; (2) a rolled blueprint with a pencil drawing a grid of rectangles, at about x 34.3 y 47.1 w 4.4 h 6.7; (3) a globe with a small gear overlapping its lower right, at about x 63.9 y 46.9 w 3.9 h 6.8. Three numbered circle nodes (1, 2, 3) with thin orange outlines sit on a horizontal orange timeline that runs left to right, bends down at the right into a lower horizontal segment that exits the right edge. Small orange vertical connectors drop from each node to an orange dot on the top edge of its card. Orange Versigent logo mark (a stylised split circle) with white wordmark at bottom right.

**Design**

Background: Solid dark navy #16283F. Dark navy background #16283F with no gradient or texture. Title and number labels are white #FFFFFF in bold Calibri sans-serif, title about 7.4% of slide height. Cards are light blue-grey #DFE9F4 rectangles with square corners and no shadow. Timeline and connectors are orange #CD7925 thin strokes. Footnote and footer text are grey #7F7F7F Calibri.

### Slide 8

**Text**

Proprietary Tool Suite a Competitive Differentiator / HARNESS / Pillars / 1 / 2 / 3 / 4 / Translation / Design / Build / Integration / iCustomer Data Translator / Seamless data transfer from OEM systems into standardized format processable in Versigent tool suite / Product Engineering / Import of translated design elements into standard format allowing design completion and verification on one intuitive interface / Manufacturing Engineering / Exports released drawing from design to manufacturing; carries out rule checks; creates board drawing and wire kits; produces work introductions; generates digital twin of manufactured harness / Velocity Container Analyzer / Viewer tool to visualize (2D and 3D) electrical architectural design, including features to integrate design changes improving productivity, quality, and timing / 8 / Investor Presentation | March 2026 | Versigent / Versigent

**Layout**

Full-width title sits at top left (x 3.1%, y 9.5%) with the HARNESS logo and the word Pillars on a row beneath it at about 19 to 26% height. A horizontal orange timeline runs across the slide at about 30% height with four numbered circles, one per column, and a curve exiting at the right edge. Four equal columns sit below with screenshot cards at 44 to 65% height, then header and body text blocks; left margin about 4% and right margin about 3%. Footer at about 94% height has the slide number at left (x 3.9%) and the footer text beside it, with the Versigent logo at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Four boxed screenshot images of software, each with a thin orange border and a dark translucent navy tint over it, dimming the image. Card 1 (Translation): a dark-grey software window with a long grey and white banded area at top, a tree panel and a file list at the bottom, dominant grey-blue. Card 2 (Design): a dark navy CAD-style electrical schematic with blue component blocks and thin light-blue connecting wires. Card 3 (Build): a dark software screen with rows of small grid tables and a tree panel at right, mostly grey and navy. Card 4 (Integration): a desaturated photograph of a laptop on a desk, screen showing a wire-harness trace in purple and blue lines, a hand at the left edge, cropped to the card and tinted dark. Timeline: a thin orange horizontal line (about 1.5 pt) passing behind four orange-outlined circles numbered 1 to 4, with orange vertical connector lines and small orange dots at each card's top edge; the line curves down to the right edge after circle 4. HARNESS logo: a white rectangle with a black HARNESS wordmark and a red dot above the I. Versigent logo: an orange swoosh icon followed by a white wordmark.

**Design**

Background: Solid navy #16283F covering the whole slide. Dark navy slide with the navy background #16283F and a slightly lighter navy tint on cards. Title in Calibri Bold white #FFFFFF, very large (about 7.4% of slide height). Column headers in Calibri Bold orange #CD7925 at about 2.6% of slide height. Body text in Calibri regular at about 2.2% of slide height, white #FFFFFF with orange #CD7925 accent phrases. Card titles in Calibri Bold white at about 3.3% of slide height, overlaid on the top-left of each card. Pillars label Calibri Bold white at about 4.4%. Numbers in the circles are Arial regular white. Shapes are thin orange outlines with square corners on cards and circle outlines on the timeline. Cards have no shadows or gradients; they have a translucent navy tint at 50% opacity.

**Layout**

Single slide with a title across the top left at about 9.5% from the top, a light text panel on the left half from about 27% to 90% of the height, and a world map with three large orange statistics and their labels on the right, arranged in three columns above the map. A footnote sits bottom left, the footer and page number sit along the bottom left, and the Versigent logo sits bottom right. Left margin is about 3% and all left-aligned text starts on the same edge.

**Visuals**

A world map illustration drawn as a flat silhouette in #213C5F on the #16283F background, spanning the right half from about x 41% to 96% and y 25% to 89%, with no outline and no shading. Six small light-blue (#6FD3EA approximately) filled circles mark Engineering Centers (6): five on the map and one in the legend. Map dots at about x 48.7 y 59.2, x 51.1 y 62.4, x 52.2 y 63.3 (west North America), x 66.6 y 60.6 (Iberia), x 70.8 y 57.6 (Central Europe), x 85.6 y 62.4 (East Asia), each about 1.2% of slide width in diameter. Legend dot at about x 42 y 77.3. Statistics are text, not charts; no other visuals. The Versigent logo is an orange swirl mark followed by the white wordmark Versigent.

**Design**

Background: Solid navy #16283F covering the whole slide. Dark navy background #16283F. Title text is white #FFFFFF Calibri Bold, large (about 7.4% of slide height for cap height ascent). Stat numbers are orange #CD7925 Calibri Bold. Panel headings are orange #CD7925 Calibri Bold at about 3.3% of slide height. Body text is black #000000 Calibri regular at about 2.6% of slide height with bold runs for emphasis. Stat labels are white #FFFFFF Calibri regular at about 2.2%. Footer and footnotes are grey #7F7F7F Calibri at about 1.8 to 2.0%. Panel is a flat rectangle filled #DFE9F4 with square corners and no border or shadow. Bullets are small orange #CD7925 round dots in ArialMT. No gradients or shadows.

**Layout**

Three-column-style layout on a dark navy field: a light left panel holds three stacked sections (Integrated, Localized, Resilient) with orange headings and bullets; the right area holds three large orange statistics across the top, a European inset map centred below-left, and an Example KSK Program bullet list at the lower right. The title sits top-left at about 9.5% from the top with a 3.1% left margin, left-aligned. A notes line sits bottom-left above the footer; the footer carries the page number 10 at bottom-left, the footer text beside it, and the Versigent logo at bottom-right.

**Visuals**

A faded world map silhouette in #213C5F fills the right two-thirds of the slide behind the statistics and the Example KSK Program list, with light grey landmass outlines at low contrast. Three large orange statistics: 95% (supplier visibility to at least Tier 3 level), >95% (cable sourced in-region), ~99% (cable value chain mapped down to copper source). A boxed inset map of Europe (#D9D9D9 countries at 20% opacity over a dark slate box with a teal-grey border) shows Germany highlighted in blue #0857C3 and Serbia highlighted in blue #0857C3. A dashed dark-green curved arrow runs from Serbia to Germany with a dark green dot at Germany (with arrowhead pointing into Germany) and a dark green dot at Serbia. An airplane glyph (Wingdings) sits between the labels Serbia and Germany on the upper-right of the inset. A dotted teal-grey rectangle outline on the world map marks the inset's zoom area, connected by a dotted line to the inset's top edge. The light left panel #DFE9F4 contains only text. The Versigent logo: an orange #CD7925 stylised swirl mark followed by the white wordmark Versigent, at lower right.

11
Investor Presentation | March 2026 | Versigent

**Layout**

Full-bleed dark navy slide with the title across the top left at about 3% from the left edge, aligned to a left margin of about 3%. Below the title, a light-blue text panel occupies the left column (about 3% to 40% of width, 26.5% to 86% of height) with three headed bullet blocks, while the right side holds three large orange statistics in a row across the top of a world map, with the manufacturing-facility legend below the left panel's right edge. Footnotes sit under the panel, footer with page number at bottom left and the Versigent logo at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Stylised world map silhouette in dark blue #213C5F spanning about 41% to 96% of width and 25% to 89% of height, sitting on the navy background with no outline. About 59 small light-blue (#62CFEF) solid dots mark manufacturing facilities: a cluster of about 12 over western North America / Mexico, a few over Western Europe and the Mediterranean, a cluster of about 6 over Central / Eastern Europe, about 3 over Western Africa / Iberia, a cluster over the Indian subcontinent (about 6), a dot cluster of about 15 over Eastern China, a few over Japan and Korea, two over Southeast Asia / Indonesia, and two dots at the southern tip of South America. Three large orange (#CD7925) statistics: '>95%', '99.9%' and '99.9%', each with a white caption beneath. A small light-blue dot legend marker precedes 'Manufacturing Facilities (59)' in white. Orange and white Versigent logo mark at bottom right. No photographs; no charts or tables.

**Design**

Background: Solid navy #16283F. Solid dark navy background #16283F. Titles white #FFFFFF in Calibri Bold, approximately 7.4% of slide height. Panel headings orange #CD7925 Calibri Bold at about 3.0% of slide height. Body text black #000000 Calibri regular at about 2.3% of slide height. Stat numerals orange #CD7925 Calibri Bold at about 7.4%; captions white Calibri regular at about 2.2%. Map fill #213C5F; facility dots #62CFEF. Light-blue text panel #DFE9F4 with square corners, no border, no shadow. Footnotes and footer in grey #7F7F7F Calibri at about 1.8% to 2.0%. Bullets are small orange '•' glyphs in Arial.

### Slide 12

**Text**

Industry / Dynamics / 12 / Investor Presentation | March 2026 | Versigent / Versigent

**Layout**

A single-slide section divider with a left-aligned two-line title set in the lower-left middle of the slide, with the ribbon illustration filling the right half and reaching to the top edge. The footer runs along the bottom edge with the page number at left, the footer text beside it, and the Versigent logo at bottom right. No header and no subtitle.

**Visuals**

One illustration: stylised gold ribbons, about 10 to 12 parallel bands per bundle, rendered with a slight 3D twist and shading. The first bundle enters at the top edge around x 49 to 67% and curves down and to the left to converge toward the lower middle around x 60 to 75%, y 70 to 86%. The second bundle enters at the top edge around x 73 to 87%, descends in an S-curve, and fans out at its lower end around x 75 to 86%, y 62 to 86%. The two bundles cross and overlap in the centre-right area. Ribbon colour runs from gold #D9A53A to amber #C8862E, with darker orange-brown #B8702A on the shaded edges and lighter #E6B84A highlights. The image is not cropped inside its box and is blended into the navy background with no boxing, border or overlay. Lighting is soft and directional from the upper left.

**Design**

Background: Solid navy #16283F across the full slide. Full navy background #16283F with no gradient or texture. Title is Calibri Bold, cream white #FBFAF6, large, about 11.1% of slide height per line, tight leading with line spacing of roughly 1.0. Footer text is Calibri regular in grey #7F7F7F at about 2.0% of slide height. Accent colour is gold #D9A53A in the ribbons and orange #D9722B in the logo mark. No shapes, rules or shadows beyond the illustration.

**Text**

Secular Trends Requiring More Robust Solutions
Product Requirements for

and performance

customers

to understand precise needs and requirements

• Providing customers with same product at same

• Localized manufacturing and resilient supply chain to

13
Investor Presentation | March 2026 | Versigent

**Layout**

Three-column grid on a full navy slide: a narrow left column of three stacked photo tiles (Automation, Electrification, Digitalization), a centre column with a blue header bar over three stacked cards, and a right column with a lighter blue header bar over two taller cards. The title sits top-left, left-aligned at about 9.5% from the top with a left margin of about 3.1%. The footer carries the page number at bottom left and the text 'Investor Presentation | March 2026 | Versigent' beside it, with the Versigent logo at bottom right; the right column ends at about 91% height and the footer sits at about 94%.

**Visuals**

Three photographic tiles in the left column, each with a navy 60% overlay and a thin orange border, each carrying a white line icon in a white circle. (1) Automation: a worker in a yellow hard hat and dark clothing at a workbench of tools and panels, mostly dark teal and warm orange tones, with a white robot-arm line icon in a circle. (2) Electrification: a close view of hands working on red-orange electrical wiring with blurred components, warm red and brown tones, with a white power-symbol line icon in a circle. (3) Digitalization: a glowing blue circuit-line network on a dark surface with purple and cyan highlights, with a white gear and circuit line icon in a circle. Card icons in thin navy-grey and orange line style: an orange gear inside an open folder above the Advanced Feature Enablement card; a navy outlined warning triangle with an orange exclamation mark above Mission Critical; an orange circular-arrow loop around a navy gear with check mark beside Optimized Design; orange-and-navy stacked layers beside End-to-End Capabilities; an orange-outlined globe with navy continents beside Global Scale and Resilient Supply Chain. Logo: an orange swoosh mark with a white 'Versigent' wordmark at bottom right.

**Design**

Background: Solid navy #16283F. Full navy background #16283F. Titles and tile labels white (#FFFFFF) Calibri Bold, sans-serif. Card titles black (#000000) Calibri Bold. Body text black (#000000) Calibri regular. Column headers white Calibri Bold on blue bars: #0857C3 for Product Requirements, #4E96F8 for Business Requirements. Cards are flat #DFE9F4 with no borders or shadows. Accents are orange #CD7925 for tile borders and bullet dots. Title is about 7.4% of slide height, card titles about 2.96%, column headers and tile labels about 3.33%, body text about 2.22%. Footer text is grey #7F7F7F Calibri about 2.04%. Shapes are square-cornered with no gradients.

### Slide 14

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Before-and-after comparisons of architecture approaches with percentage weight and labor savings let the reader compare the two design options directly.
- Review: Slides 4, 14 and 28 show strong hierarchy, readable key numbers and clean charts, and the bridges on slide 28 make the 2025 and 2026 story easy to follow.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge or cut the body text and footnotes on slides 9, 11 and 14, and fix the 2026 bridge on slide 28 so the small $0.0 steps and bar proportions read clearly.

14
Investor Presentation | March 2026 | Versigent

**Layout**

Single full-width slide with a bold title across the top-left and a three-column card grid below a wide two-part header band (Domain Architecture left, Zonal Architecture right), with 'Incremental Approach' left-aligned and 'Clean-Sheet Approach' right-aligned beneath the band. Notes text sits bottom-left, footer line with page number 14 at bottom-left and the Versigent logo at bottom-right, all on margins of about 3 percent of slide width.

**Visuals**

Three light-blue (#DFE9F4) rectangular cards, each with a small translucent light-blue (#89B9FA at 50 percent opacity) downward-pointing triangle beneath its orange headline figure: Card 1 'Process-Only1 / Automating 19% of manufacturing labor hours' with bullets, Card 2 'Design Optimization / ~10% weight reduction', Card 3 'Fully Optimized Design / ~30% weight reduction'. A wide gradient wedge (rising triangle, about 901 by 85 pixels) runs from bottom-left to top-right across the header band: amber/tan (#D9B47A) at the bottom-left fading through dark brown (#5A3A12) to charcoal (#2E2E2E) at the top-right corner, and it sits behind the 'Leading Truck / SUV Platform' and 'High Performance SUV' labels. Two translucent white (#FFFFFF at 50 percent opacity) header bars sit behind 'Domain Architecture' (left, wider) and 'Zonal Architecture' (right). Versigent logo at bottom-right: an orange (#E07B1F) stylised swirl mark with the white wordmark 'Versigent' in sans-serif. No photographs or charts.

**Design**

Background: Solid dark navy #16283F. Solid dark navy background #16283F. Title white (#FFFFFF) Calibri Bold, about 7.4 percent of slide height. Card headings black (#000000) Calibri regular about 3.3 percent; orange emphasis figures #CD7925 Calibri Bold about 3.7 percent; bullet body black Calibri regular about 2.6 percent; bullets are orange #C47900 ArialMT dots. Header labels: 'Leading Truck / SUV Platform' and 'High Performance SUV' in #C47900 Calibri Bold 4.4 percent; 'Domain Architecture' #004624 dark green Calibri Bold; 'Zonal Architecture' #0857C3 blue Calibri Bold. Approach labels white Calibri 2.6 percent. Footnotes and footer in grey #7F7F7F Calibri about 1.8 to 2.0 percent. Shapes are square-cornered with no shadows or outlines; the only gradient is the header wedge.

15
Investor Presentation | March 2026 | Versigent

**Layout**

Single-slide content layout on a full-bleed dark navy field with the title across the top-left at about 9.5% from the top, left-aligned at about 3% margin. Three vehicle columns (ICE, HYBRID, BEV) sit in the upper-middle band with blue header bars, with the car illustrations below each header, and a stepped green bar stack with light-blue low-voltage bars occupies the lower band from the left-hand legend column to the right margin; the notes sit below the bars, with the page number and footer bottom-left and the Versigent logo bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Three line-art style car illustrations rendered as translucent light-blue wireframe vehicles showing battery packs, drive units and wiring in a 3/4 top-down view, each car boxed with no frame. Left car (ICE, under 1X): smaller compact silhouette with a small engine block and exhaust visible in the front-centre, facing right. Middle car (HYBRID, under 1.5X): similar silhouette with a mid-sized battery pack and motor visible, facing right. Right car (BEV, under 1.7X): larger rectangular battery pack spanning the floor, electric drive unit visible at the rear, facing right and overlapping the top edge of the green triangle. Lighting is flat and uniform on the dark background; the cars are pale cyan (about #8FD8E8) with low opacity. Hybrid connector line: a thin orange (#CD7925) horizontal line of about 0.2% stroke running from MILD to PLUG-IN, interrupted around the FULL label. Green wedge: a right-triangle with its hypotenuse rising from lower left to upper right, dark green fill #00723B, overlapping the BEV car base and containing the High voltage electrical architectures text. Green Cord sets bar and light-blue bars are flat rectangles with no shadow or outline. Versigent logo in bottom-right: an orange (#CD7925) stylised V-shaped mark and white wordmark 'Versigent'. Orange outlined rectangle (about 0.2% stroke, #CD7925) enclosing the rotated text Versigent Content on the left legend column.

**Design**

Background solid dark navy #16283F with no gradient or texture. Titles white (#FFFFFF) Calibri Bold, large, about 7.4% of slide height, sans-serif. Body and labels white Calibri at about 2.6% of slide height; header labels white Calibri Bold about 3.7%; orange accent #CD7925 for the large multiplier figures (about 5.6%, Calibri Bold) and Versigent Content label. Blue header bars #064192; High Voltage box #004624 dark green; Low Voltage box #0857C3 bright blue; green bars #00723B; light-blue bars #C4DCFD with black Calibri text. Footnote and footer grey #7F7F7F Calibri about 1.9% to 2.0%. Shapes are square-cornered, no shadows, no gradients.

16
Investor Presentation | March 2026 | Versigent
Versigent

**Layout**

Single slide on a dark navy field with the title across the top left at about 9.5% from the top, spanning most of the width. The left column (about 3% to 45% horizontally) holds an italic chart caption and a two-bar column chart sitting on a baseline at about 73% of the height. The right column (about 50% to 97%) stacks a blue header bar, a green positive-drivers panel, a red negative-drivers panel, a white divider line and an orange net-result bar, all left-aligned to a common edge. Footnotes sit at about 88% to 92% height, with the page number and footer text at about 94% height on the left and the Versigent logo bottom right. Margins are about 3% left and 3% right.

**Visuals**

Column chart with title 'Versigent Addressable Content per Vehicle ($ CPV)' with superscript 1. Two columns: 2025 Baseline, value $740, grey #4B545F, bar spanning about 7.6% to 19.5% horizontally and 48.5% to 73.4% vertically; 2030 Optimized, value $770, orange #CD7925, bar spanning about 28.5% to 40.4% horizontally and 43.2% to 73.4% vertically. Printed values are $740 and $770, no axis or scale is printed, and the bar heights are not labelled further. A thin white baseline runs under both columns. A white arrow with a white arrowhead runs from about (14.9%, 56.1%) up and right to about (33.3%, 48.4%) over the gap between the bars. A white-filled oval with a gold outline sits on the arrow at about 21.9% to 26.4% horizontally and 48.6% to 56.2% vertically, reading '1%' above 'CAGR' in black bold. Right-hand panel: a blue header bar reading 'Drivers of Content per Vehicle Growth'; below it a dark green panel (#00B45D at 30% opacity over navy, appearing about #0E4F40) listing four items each preceded by a white circle with a dark green plus sign; below that a dark maroon panel (#C00000 at 30% opacity, appearing about #471C2A) listing two items each preceded by a white circle with a dark red minus bar; a thin white horizontal divider at about 71% height; and an orange #CD7925 bar reading '+1% NET CAGR IN CPV OVER FORECAST PERIOD' preceded by a white circle with an orange equals sign. Logo at bottom right: an orange swirl mark with the white wordmark Versigent.

**Design**

Background solid dark navy #16283F with no gradient, texture or photograph. Titles are white #FFFFFF Calibri Bold, sans-serif, about 7.4% of slide height for the main title. Body and list text is white Calibri regular about 3.0% of slide height. The subtitle is white Calibri Italic about 2.6%. The header and CTA-bar label are white Calibri Bold about 3.3%. Chart values and category labels are white Arial Bold about 2.6%, and the CAGR label is black Calibri Bold. Accent colours: orange #CD7925 for the 2030 bar and result bar, grey #4B545F for the baseline bar, blue #064192 for the header, green and maroon tinted panels. Footer and notes text is grey #7F7F7F Calibri about 1.8% to 2.0%. Shapes are square-cornered rectangles with no shadows. The arrow and divider are thin white strokes. Icons are white filled circles.

17
Investor Presentation | March 2026 | Versigent

**Layout**

Single slide with a title band across the top left aligned at about 3% from the left edge. Below it, two columns: a light rounded-corner-free panel on the left (about 5% to 46% of the width, 28% to 86% of the height) holding two labelled lists with icons, and a 3-row photo grid on the right (about 54% to 94% of the width, 26% to 86% of the height) with a thin orange-to-dark gradient arrow between them at about 48% to 51% of the width. Footer at the bottom left holds the page number 17 and the text Investor Presentation | March 2026 | Versigent, and the Versigent logo sits at the bottom right. Margins are about 4% left and right.

**Visuals**

Four photographs and one gradient arrow, plus the icons in the left panel. Top right photo (full row width, about 55% to 95% of the width, 28% to 47% of the height): a white-cab semi truck with a grey trailer driving left to right on a highway, green trees behind, guardrail below, daytime, cool neutral light, dominant colours grey, white and green; slightly darkened by a navy tint overlay; boxed with square corners and cropped at the sides. Middle left photo (about 55% to 80% of the width, 48% to 67% of the height): a red and green farm tractor in a field at sunset, sun on the horizon right of centre, warm orange and amber sky, tilled brown soil in the foreground, shallow composition from a front-three-quarter angle; navy tint overlay. Middle right photo (about 80% to 94% of the width, 48% to 67% of the height): rows of white battery energy storage containers on a flat site with power line towers in the background at dusk, pale grey and blue-violet sky; navy tint overlay. Bottom left photo (about 55% to 75% of the width, 67% to 86% of the height): a yellow crawler bulldozer on a dirt pile under a pale blue sky, side view; navy tint overlay. Bottom right photo (about 75% to 94% of the width, 67% to 86% of the height): a close-up of a white humanoid robot head in profile looking left, with blurred blue, violet and white light streaks in the background; navy tint overlay. Gradient arrow (about 48.5% to 51.7% of the width, 27.6% to 86% of the height): a tapered chevron-like band that is dark navy at its left edge and fades to bright orange #CD7925 at its right point, pointing right toward the photos. Icons in the left panel are thin single-weight line icons in dark navy with orange accents: a gear inside a box with three circles on top (for Support increasing features and functions); a warning triangle with an exclamation mark (for Demonstrate mission critical reliability); a gear inside a circular arrow with a check mark (for Optimized design); stacked layers (for High performing, cost effective solutions); and a globe showing continents (for Global scale & supply chain). Versigent logo in the bottom right: an orange rounded swirl mark followed by the white wordmark Versigent in a sans-serif face.

**Design**

Background: Solid navy #16283F. Dark navy background #16283F across the whole slide. Title in white #FFFFFF Calibri Bold, sans-serif. Section headers Product Requirements, Business Requirements and Strategic Focus Markets in orange #CD7925 Calibri Bold. Body items in black #000000 Calibri regular on a light blue-grey panel #DFE9F4 with no border, square corners and no shadow. Footer text is grey #7F7F7F Calibri regular. Title about 7.4% of slide height; section headers about 3.3%; body and list text about 3.0%; footer about 2.0%. Photos are boxed with hairline gaps of dark navy between them and have a navy tint overlay that dims them. The gradient arrow is the only gradient on the slide.

18
Investor Presentation | March 2026 | Versigent

**Layout**

Dark full-slide layout with the title spanning the top band left-aligned at about 3% from the left edge. Three columns: left column holds a single 2025 bar with its label, a centre column holds a stacked 2030E bar with its label, and the right column holds three stacked horizontal panels with thumbnail photos at the right of each panel, all sitting between about 24% and 90% of slide height. A footnote block sits at the bottom left above a footer line with the page number 18 at the far left and the Versigent logo at the bottom right.

**Visuals**

Two stacked bar-style columns plus a CAGR callout and three panels with icons and photos. Bar chart: unlabelled axis, no axis scale printed, baseline rules under each bar. Bar 2025 (about 2025 total): single grey (#7F7F7F) segment labelled ~$70, unit $ Billions. Bar 2030E: total labelled ~$145, stacked bottom to top: grey #7F7F7F segment (largest, labelled none), dark green #004624 segment (commercial vehicle), teal #1A7E94 segment split by two thin darker horizontal lines into upper teal segments (other industrials). No legend. Highlighted connector: orange arrow from the 2025 bar to the 2030E bar labelled 2% CAGR inside a white circle with orange outline. Dashed connectors from the stacked segments to the three panels: teal dashed line from teal segment to OTHER INDUSTRIALS panel, green dashed line from green segment to COMMERCIAL VEHICLE panel, grey dashed line from grey segment to AUTOMOTIVE panel. Panels: OTHER INDUSTRIALS 7% with a teal factory-conveyor line icon and photo of modular battery storage containers beside wind turbines and a city skyline, muted grey-blue tones, boxed full-height at right of panel; COMMERCIAL VEHICLE 3% with a green truck line icon and photo of a white semi-trailer truck on a highway with green trees, daytime, boxed; AUTOMOTIVE 2% with a grey car-with-signal-waves line icon and photo of a dark car driving fast down a road with blurred green landscape, daytime, boxed. Logo: Versigent wordmark in white with an orange swirl mark at bottom right.

**Design**

Background: Solid dark navy #16283F full-bleed.

**Layout**

Single 16:9 slide with the title 'Solid Revenue Growth Profile' set flush left across the top (left edge about 3.2% of width, baseline band about 9.5% to 17% of height) with no subtitle. Two equal-height light panels sit side by side in the lower band, from about 27.7% to 85.6% of height: a left chart panel (x 3.3% to 48.7%) and a right text panel (x 49.6% to 95.0%), with a gutter of about 0.9% between them and content left-aligned inside each. Footnotes sit left-aligned below the panels at about 88% to 92% of height, the footer line and slide number sit at about 94% of height at the bottom left, and the Versigent logo sits bottom right at about 92% to 97% of height.

**Visuals**

Chart: vertical bar chart titled 'Revenue' with unit label '$ Billions' (grey italic, no axis scale or gridlines shown). Three series-free bars with printed values: 2022 bar labelled $8.0, 2025 bar labelled $8.8, 2028E bar labelled ~$10 (the ~ marks an approximate value). Bars are solid blue #0857C3 with no outline, sitting on a thin dark horizontal baseline (about #1A1A1A, roughly 0.2% of height) spanning about x 5.1% to 45.2%. Category labels 2022, 2025, 2028E sit centred beneath each bar in black Calibri. No legend. No highlighted points. Two annotation callouts: a circle with orange outline (#CD7925, about 0.2% of height stroke) above the gap between 2022 and 2025 containing '3% / Adj.1 / CAGR' in black bold, joined to an orange arrow (#CD7925, open arrowhead, about 0.3% stroke) running up and to the right from the 2022 bar top area to just above the 2025 bar; a second identical-style circle above the gap between 2025 and 2028E containing '3-4% / Adj.1 / CAGR' with an orange arrow running up and right toward the 2028E label area. Circle centres are about x 22.0% y 46.8% (3% callout, diameter about 4.7% of width) and x 31.5% y 43.9% (3-4% callout). Arrows: one from about x 12.3% y 49.0% to x 23.8% y 43.6%, the second from about x 25.8% y 46.1% to x 37.3% y 41.0%. Bar boxes: 2022 bar about x 8.0% to 15.5% wide with top near 53.9% of height; 2025 bar about x 21.4% to 28.9% with top near 51.3%; 2028E bar x 34.8% to 42.3%, top at 48.0%, bottom at the baseline 80.0%. The file measurement for one bar (x 8.0%, w 20.9%, y 51.3%, h 28.7%) does not match the visible bar widths and is recorded in the elements list as read. Photographs or illustrations: none. Logo: Versigent logo, an orange (#CD7925) stylised double-chevron mark followed by the white wordmark 'Versigent' in a sans-serif, bottom right.

**Design**

Background: Solid dark navy #16283F covering the whole slide. Dark navy full-bleed background (#16283F) with light-blue-grey panels (#DFE9F4) carrying the content. Title text is white (#FFFFFF) Calibri Bold, about 7.4% of slide height, sentence-free title case. Panel headings and section heads ('Revenue', '2025–2028E:', 'Bookings', 'Drivers') are burnt orange #CD7925 Calibri Bold, about 3.0% to 3.3% of slide height. Body text is black #000000 Calibri regular, about 2.2% of slide height, with orange #CD7925 bullets. Chart bars are strong blue #0857C3. Units line is grey #7F7F7F Calibri italic, about 2.6%. Footnotes and footer text are grey #7F7F7F Calibri regular, about 1.8% to 2.0% of height. Callout circles have white fill with orange outline. Panels are square-cornered flat rectangles with no shadow or gradient. Overall style is a corporate investor deck: sans-serif, flat, high contrast.

Drivers

Continued improvement in mix of revenue and programs, incl. full service
Material, manufacturing and SG&A performance, partially offset by labor economics
Cost Structure Actions
Material performance driven by engineering design changes and vendor negotiations
Manufacturing performance driven by footprint consolidation and rotation, and increased automation, partially offset by labor inflation
SG&A & Other reduced through overhead cost-reduction initiatives, partially offset by investments to support Non-Auto growth
Engineering productivity driven by resource consolidation in best-cost countries and increased digitalization, partially offset by ongoing product and process investments
Note: 1. EBITDA margin reflects EBITDA adjusted for restructuring and other special items.

22
Investor Presentation | March 2026 | Versigent

**Layout**

Dark navy slide with the title across the top left at the margin, below which two equal light-blue panels sit side by side in two columns (left chart panel, right text panel), spanning the width with roughly 3% side margins and aligned top and bottom edges. Footnotes sit under the left panel, with the page number, footer text at bottom left and the Versigent logo at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Bar chart titled 'EBITDA Margin' with superscript 1, subtitle '% of Revenue', three vertical bars with no printed y-axis or gridlines and a thin dark baseline. Bars (left to right): 2022 labelled 9.4% (height about 24 percent of slide height), 2025 labelled 10.1% (about 26 percent), 2028E labelled ~12% (about 31 percent). Bars are solid blue #0857C3 with flat tops and no outline. Two orange (#CD7925) arrows run above the bars: the first from above the 2022 bar to above the 2025 bar, annotated by a white circle with orange outline reading '70 BPS'; the second from the 2025 bar to above the 2028E bar, annotated by a white circle with orange outline reading '~200 BPS'. No legend. No highlighted points beyond the annotated step-ups. Orange Versigent logo at bottom right: an orange stylised V-shaped mark next to white wordmark 'Versigent'.

**Design**

Background: Solid navy #16283F. Dark navy background #16283F. Title white #FFFFFF Calibri Bold, large (about 7.4 percent of slide height), sentence case. Panel headings and bullet markers in orange #CD7925 Calibri Bold (about 3.3 percent for the chart heading, 3.0 percent for section headings). Body text black #000000 Calibri regular about 2.2 percent of slide height. Subtitle and notes in grey #7F7F7F Calibri italic or regular. Panels filled #DFE9F4 with square corners and no border. Bullets are small orange round dots in ArialMT. Callout circles white with orange outline. Bars blue #0857C3.

**Layout**

Two-column layout below a full-width title: a single tall light panel on the left (about x 3.6–32.5%, y 33.5–79.3%) holds the headline figure, and a vertical stack of four equal-width light boxes on the right (x 39.3–96.9%) lists the four capital allocation priorities, with an orange gradient arrow band between them pointing right. The title sits top-left at x 3.1%, y 9.5%, left-aligned, with a left margin of about 3% and a right margin of about 3%. Footnotes sit bottom-left above the footer line, the footer and slide number sit bottom-left, and the Versigent logo sits bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Non-text elements: (1) Background: faint thin curved grey-bronze arc lines sweeping from the bottom-left up and out to the top-right over the navy field, low opacity, behind all content. (2) Orange gradient arrow band: a vertical translucent band at about x 34.4–38.2%, y 32.2–79.6%, bright orange #CD7925 at its centre fading to transparent at its top and bottom, with a point on its right edge aimed at the right-hand boxes; it sits between the left panel and the right boxes. (3) Light panel on the left holding the 'Free Cash Flow' heading and the figure '~$1B' with caption 'Cumulative FCF Generation During 2026E to 2028E'. (4) Four light rectangular boxes stacked on the right, each with an orange heading and black bullet points. (5) Versigent logo, bottom right: an orange stylised swirl mark followed by the white wordmark 'Versigent'. No charts, tables or photographs.

**Design**

Background: Solid navy #16283F with faint curved grey-bronze arc lines. Dark navy background #16283F with faint bronze arc lines. Titles are white #FFFFFF Calibri Bold, about 7.4% of slide height (about 40 pt) for the slide title, and orange #CD7925 Calibri Bold, about 3.3% of slide height (about 18 pt), for the box headings. Body text is black #000000 Calibri regular, about 2.6% of slide height (about 14 pt). Panels and boxes are light blue-grey #DFE9F4 with square corners, no visible border and no shadow. Bullets are small orange #CD7925 round dots in ArialMT. The footnotes and footer are grey #7F7F7F Calibri regular at about 1.8–2.0% of slide height (about 10–11 pt). The headline figure '~$1B' is orange #CD7925 Calibri Bold, about 9–13% of slide height. Gradient arrow band in orange fading to transparent.

**Layout**

Single slide with a title at top left (left edge about 4.6% of width, cap top about 9.5% of height) and a three-row comparison grid below it. The left column holds row labels, the centre column (x 30.3 to 62.8%) is a peach-filled FY 2026 guidance column spanning y 28.1 to 84.1%, and the right column (x 64.7 to 94.7%) holds a grey FY 2025 Carve Out header with grey values beneath. Two thin gradient rules at y 50.2% and y 67.2% divide the rows. Three lines of footnotes sit at y 86.6 to 92.8%, the footer line with page number 24 sits at y 94.3%, and the Versigent logo sits bottom right.

**Visuals**

Non-photographic slide with no charts or photographs. Visual elements: (1) a full-bleed navy background rectangle #16283F; (2) a peach rectangle #EFC9A4 behind the FY 2026 column, x 30.3%, y 28.1%, w 32.5%, h 56.0%, with no border, square corners and no shadow; (3) a light grey rectangle #D9D9D9 behind the FY 2025 Carve Out header, x 64.7%, y 28.1%, w 30.0%, h 6.7%, square corners; (4) two horizontal divider rules, each about 0.6% of slide height thick and about 89.7% wide, running from left to right with a gradient from saturated blue #1F5BD6 at the left end to white #FFFFFF at the right end, placed at y 50.2% (x 4.4%) and y 67.2% (x 4.5%), overlapping the peach column; (5) the Versigent logo at bottom right, consisting of an orange #E07B26 stylised mark resembling a split circle or leaf and the white wordmark Versigent in a sans-serif typeface, x about 85.6%, y about 92.4%, w about 11.3%, h about 4.4%.

**Design**

Background: Solid navy #16283F filling the whole slide. Dark navy background #16283F throughout with white #FFFFFF titles and labels, black #000000 values inside the peach column, grey #A6A6A6 values in the right column, grey #7F7F7F for the units note, footnotes and footer, and orange #E07B26 for the logo mark. Typeface is Calibri (sans-serif): Calibri Bold for the title and all values, Calibri regular for row labels, Calibri Italic for sub-labels and percentages. Title is about 7.4% of slide height (large), body and labels about 3.7%, sub-labels about 3.0%, footnotes about 1.8%. Divider rules are thin gradient lines. Shapes have square corners, no shadows and no rounded edges.

### Slide 25

**Text**

Conclusion: Positioned for Sustainable Value Creation / Benefiting from secular growth tailwinds / • Driving demands for increased vehicle electrification and vehicle content / Leveraging a comprehensive low- and high-voltage product portfolio / • Augmented by unique capabilities from architecture optimization to execution / Differentiated by an industry-leading operating model / • Industry-leading engineering capabilities, global manufacturing scale, and resilient supply chain / Resulting in an attractive financial profile / • Enabled by best-in-class operational execution / Driving a disciplined capital allocation strategy / • Incremental opportunity to increase shareholder value / 25 / Investor Presentation | March 2026 | Versigent

**Layout**

Single-column stacked list on a full-bleed dark slide. The title sits top-left, starting at about x 3.1% and spanning nearly the full width at y 9.5%. Below it, five equal-pitch rows run down the left half: a circular icon at x 8.6% with a bold orange heading and one white bullet line to its right, text left-aligned at x 17%. A thin orange bracket connector runs down the left margin at about x 6% and links the five icons. The footer sits at the bottom left with the page number at x 3.9% and the footer text at x 7.6%. The Versigent logo sits bottom right.

**Visuals**

Five circular line icons, one per row, each on a pale blue-white disc. Icon 1 (top): a blue circle-arrow and line chart with rising trend line and an orange tag or label shape. Icon 2: an orange rectangular box with blue circuit-style nodes and a small gear, linked by blue lines. Icon 3: a blue gear with an orange dollar sign inside a blue ring, with four orange small circular nodes on the ring at top, bottom, left and right. Icon 4: a blue rising trend arrow above orange vertical bar lines. Icon 5: an orange donut or pie chart with one segment pulled out, drawn in orange with blue detail. All icons are thin outline strokes. A decorative background image of translucent grey-brown curved arcs sweeps from the lower left up to the upper right behind the content and overlaps the text area at low opacity. A thin orange bracket line with small orange dot terminals connects the five icons along the left side.

**Design**

Background: Solid navy #16283F, with faint translucent grey-brown curved arcs overlaid across the slide. Dark navy background #16283F. Title in white #FFFFFF Calibri Bold, large, about 7.4% of slide height, sentence case. Row headings in orange #CD7925 Calibri Bold, about 3.7% of slide height. Bullet body text in white #FFFFFF Calibri regular, about 3.0% of slide height, with orange #CD7925 bullet dots. Footer text grey #7F7F7F Calibri, about 2.0% of slide height. Icon discs are flat pale blue-white #DFE9F4 circles with no outline. Icon strokes are blue #1F5FBF and orange #CD7925. Connector line is thin orange #CD7925, about 0.2% stroke. Background arcs are semi-transparent grey-brown lines, low contrast. No shadows, no corner radii on text, no gradient on the base fill.

### Slide 26

**Text**

Appendix / 26 / Investor Presentation | March 2026 | Versigent / Versigent

**Layout**

A full-bleed navy slide with the gold line illustration across the upper half and a large bold title left-aligned at about 4.7% from the left edge, just below the illustration. A footer row sits at the bottom with the page number at the left margin and the footer line beside it, and the logo sits at the bottom right. There is no visible grid or column structure beyond the left-aligned title.

**Visuals**

One illustration: a set of about 14 thin, smooth, gold-amber strands running horizontally from the left edge to the right edge, each with S-shaped step transitions. In the upper group, strands rise from the left and step up near the top centre, while two strands step up toward the top right and one stays low; in the middle group, strands cross near the centre-left and step up or down. The strands are fine, roughly 1 to 2 pixels wide, with a slight fade toward the lower right where the lowest strand ends at about x 53% and continues faintly to the right edge. The illustration is full width and occupies the top 51.4% of the slide, bleeding off the left, right and top edges. No photograph, chart or table. Logo: an orange (#E07B24) abstract emblem of two curved shapes followed by the white wordmark Versigent, at the bottom right.

**Design**

Background solid deep navy #16283F. Title 'Appendix' in white #FFFFFF, Calibri Bold (a humanist sans-serif), large, approximately 11.1% of slide height in cap-height terms about 7.8% for the capital A, with normal letter spacing. Footer text in Calibri regular, about 2.0% of slide height, grey #7F7F7F. Illustration strands in gold-amber around #D9A441 with slight transparency. Logo emblem orange #E07B24, wordmark white #FFFFFF. Lines have no shadows, gradients or rounded shape styles; no boxes or borders.

### Slide 27

**Text**

Today’s Presenters
Joseph Liotine is a seasoned public company executive with more than two decades of leadership at global publicly traded companies. He brings a proven track record of successfully guiding companies through transformation and growth, driving operational excellence, and deepening customer relationships through innovation. His strategic acumen and operational expertise, backed by strong financial knowledge and extensive experience across manufacturing, supply chain management, marketing, sales, and technology, position him to lead Versigent as it builds on its 100-year legacy of innovation and trusted OEM partnerships.
Mr. Liotine currently serves as Executive Vice President and President, Electrical Distribution Systems at Aptiv PLC, a position he has held since November 2024. In this role, he leads Aptiv's premier vehicle architecture product line, driving industry adoption of optimized low and high-voltage vehicle architectures. His responsibilities include leading the execution of the EDS global strategy, with a focus on footprint optimization, enhanced automation, and growth in automotive and adjacent markets.
Mr. Liotine joined Aptiv in April 2024 as President, Signal and Power Solutions, where he was responsible for driving the seamless alignment and execution of commercial and operating strategies within S&PS globally, including Electrical Distribution Systems, Connection Systems, Vehicle Electrification Systems, HellermannTyton, Winchester Interconnect, and Intercable Automotive Solutions.
Prior to joining Aptiv, Mr. Liotine served as Chief Executive Officer at Briggs & Stratton, the world's largest producer of engines for outdoor power equipment and the leading provider of job site products across its brand portfolio. Previously, he spent nearly 20 years in senior executive roles at Whirlpool Corporation, most recently as President and Chief Operating Officer, where he led the global appliance business. Mr. Liotine began his career at PepsiCo, where he excelled in several financial management roles within sales and procurement.
Mr. Liotine graduated from Illinois State University with a Bachelor of Science in Finance, International Business, and Economics. He also holds a Master of Business Administration with a concentration in Marketing, Finance, and Entrepreneurship from the University of Chicago Booth School of Business.
Joe Liotine
Chief Executive Officer
Douglas Ostermann is a highly experienced finance executive with more than 30 years of leadership in global finance, treasury, and strategy across the automotive and industrials sectors. His proven track record of driving financial results at leading global companies positions him to guide Versigent's financial organization and create long-term shareholder value.
Mr. Ostermann is currently Vice President, Finance, Electrical Distribution Systems at Aptiv, a position he has held since December 2025. In this role, he oversees all financial activities for the business segment.
Most recently, Mr. Ostermann served as Group Chief Financial Officer, Head of Global Financial Services and Head of Joint Ventures and M&A at Stellantis. Prior to this role, he spent three years based in Shanghai, first as Chief Financial Officer and Head of Strategy for the China Region, then as Chief Operating Officer for China. He also held senior finance leadership roles at Archer Daniels Midland Company and spent over a decade at General Motors Company in treasury, business development, and marketing roles.
Mr. Ostermann holds a Bachelor of Science in Business Administration and a Master of Business Administration from the John M. Olin School of Business at Washington University in St. Louis.
Doug Ostermann
Chief Financial Officer

**Layout**

Single-slide layout on a full dark navy field with the title top-left at about 9% from the top and a 3% left margin. Two presenter rows stack vertically: a boxed headshot with name and role beneath it at left (x about 3%), and a justified-left body text column starting at x 17% and running to about 97% for each presenter. Footer runs along the bottom with the page number at left, the footer text beside it, and the Versigent logo at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Two boxed headshot photographs of men, each framed by a thin orange border. Top photo (Joe Liotine): head-and-shoulders portrait of a bald man with clear rectangular glasses, short greyish-brown beard and a broad smile, wearing a navy suit jacket over an open-collar white shirt, facing the camera, against a blurred bright indoor background with soft frontal lighting; dominant colours navy, white and warm grey; not cropped to a circle, shown in a full rectangular box. Bottom photo (Doug Ostermann): head-and-shoulders portrait of a smiling man with short greying brown hair, dark navy suit and white open-collar shirt, against a blurred grey-blue background with soft even lighting; dominant colours navy, slate grey and white; rectangular box with orange border. Versigent logo at bottom right: an orange stylised V-swoosh mark (#D9822B) followed by the white wordmark 'Versigent' in a lowercase sans-serif.

**Design**

Background: Solid dark navy #16283F covering the entire slide. Solid dark navy background (#16283F). Title in bold Calibri-style humanist sans-serif, white #FFFFFF, about 8.2% of slide height, cap height large. Body text in regular Calibri-style sans-serif, off-white #FBFAF6, about 1.84% of slide height, with roughly 2% line pitch and paragraph gaps. Footer text grey #7F7F7F at about 2% of slide height. Photo borders thin orange lines, square corners, no shadow, no gradients.

### Slide 28

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Side-by-side revenue and adjusted EBITDA bridges show the 2025 actual, the drivers of change, and the 2026 guidance in matching layouts, which makes the year-over-year story easy to follow.
- Review: Slides 4, 14 and 28 show strong hierarchy, readable key numbers and clean charts, and the bridges on slide 28 make the 2025 and 2026 story easy to follow.
- Review: Slide 28 repeats nearly identical bridges with $0.0 labels and vague bar scaling, which weakens the chart, and the deck reads better as a read-ahead than as a projected talk.
- Worth keeping: Keep the navy and orange identity, the large orange or blue key figures with short labels (slides 4, 6, 10), and the paired bridge structure on slide 28.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge or cut the body text and footnotes on slides 9, 11 and 14, and fix the 2026 bridge on slide 28 so the small $0.0 steps and bar proportions read clearly.

**Layout**

Four equal chart panels sit in a 2 by 2 grid below a full-width title, with the 2025 charts in the top row and the 2026 charts in the bottom row, and the left column of panels on the left half and the right column on the right half. The title sits top-left at about 9.5% from the top, left-aligned at x 3.1%. Footnotes sit bottom-left under the panels at about 89% to 92% height, the footer line with slide number 28 sits at about 94% height at the bottom-left, and the Versigent logo sits bottom-right; there is a narrow gutter of about 4% between panels and margins of about 3% left and right.

**Visuals**

Four waterfall-style bridge charts drawn with rectangles and lines, not chart objects, each with a percentage callout circle joined to its start and end bars by an orange bracket line. Top-left: 2025 REVENUE, $ Millions. Bars: 2024 blue column $8,309; Sales Growth (3) green floating bar $372; FX and Commodities green floating bar $137; 2025 blue column $8,818; callout 4% Adj1 in a circle with orange bracket from 2024 to 2025. Top-right: 2025 Adj. EBITDA (2), $ Millions. Bars: 2024 blue $776; Sales Growth (3) green $70; Performance, Net Labor Econ green $121; FX and Commodities orange floating bar ($74); 2025 blue $893; callout 15% in a circle with orange bracket. Bottom-left: 2026 REVENUE, $ Billions. Bars: 2025 blue $8.8; Sales Growth (3) green $0.2; FX and Commodities green $0.3; 2026E blue ~$9.25; callout 3% Adj1. Bottom-right: 2026 Adj. EBITDA (2), $ Billions. Bars: 2025 blue $0.9; Sales Growth (3) green $0.0; Performance, Net Labor Econ green $0.1; FX and Commodities orange ($0.0); 2026E blue ~$1.0; callout 9% in a circle. Category labels sit under each bar; no axes, gridlines or legend are printed. Orange dots mark the bracket ends. No photographs or icons apart from the logo.

**Design**

Background: Solid navy #16283F covering the whole slide. Dark navy full-slide background #16283F with light blue-grey panels #DFE9F4 behind each chart. Title text white #FFFFFF in Calibri Bold, about 7.4% of slide height, sentence-style. Panel headings black #000000 in Calibri Bold about 3.0% of slide height; unit labels grey #7F7F7F in Calibri Bold Italic about 2.2%. Category and value labels black #000000 Calibri about 2.0%; value labels inside bars white #FFFFFF Calibri about 2.0%. Accent blue #0857C3 for revenue columns and percentage text; dark green #004624 for positive bars; amber #F9A900 for negative bars; orange #D98A2B for bracket lines, dots and callout circle outlines. Footnotes grey #7F7F7F Calibri about 1.8%. Shapes are flat with no corner radius, shadow or gradient.

### Slide 29

**Text**

Definitions to Non- GAAP Measures & Reconciliations
29
Investor Presentation | March 2026 | Versigent
•
Adjusted Revenue defined as Revenue adjusted for FX and Commodities.
•
Adjusted EBITDA defined as EBITDA adjusted for restructuring and special items.
•
Adjusted EBITDA Margin defined as Adjusted EBITDA as a % of Revenue.
•
Gross Leverage defined as Gross Debt divided by Adjusted EBITDA.
•
Free Cash Flow defined as Operating Cash Flow less Capital Expenditures, excluding impact of
intercompany transfers of intellectual property and other related items.
•
Free Cash Flow Conversion defined as Free Cash Flow as a % of Net Income.

**Layout**

A single left-aligned text column on a plain white slide. The title sits across the top at about 9.5% from the top edge, left edge at about 3.1%, spanning nearly the full width. Bullet markers sit at about 5% from the left edge with bullet text starting at about 7.8%, stacked in six items from about 27.8% to 63% of the height. The footer runs along the bottom: slide number at about 3.9% from the left, footer text beside it at about 7.6%, both at about 94.3% from the top. The Versigent logo sits bottom right. There is no slide-number-based grid, no image and no box.

**Visuals**

none apart from the Versigent logo at bottom right: an orange stylised leaf-like mark (two interlocking curved shapes in orange #D27A2A and dark tones) followed by the wordmark 'Versigent' in black sans-serif lowercase-style lettering.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background #FFFFFF with no gradient, pattern or photograph. Title in Calibri Bold, black #000000, sentence-style capitalisation, about 7.4% of slide height. Body in Calibri regular, black #000000, about 3.7% of slide height, with generous spacing between bullets. Bullet markers are small round dots in ArialMT, orange #CD7925. Footer text is Calibri regular grey #7F7F7F at about 2% of slide height. Accent colour is orange #CD7925 used for bullets and logo. No shapes, lines, rules, shadows, corner radii or gradients.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

- shape at x 0, y 0, w 100, h 100 [#FFFFFF]: Full-slide background fill, solid white (Covers entire slide, no border, no shadow.)

**Layout**

Three stacked tables in a single left-aligned column with a common left margin of about 3% of slide width, each under its own bold title, with the title of the first table at top and the last table ending near 90% of slide height. Tables are full-width bands in the left 84% of the slide, with numeric columns right-aligned under year headers. Footer line with page number 30 and the text Investor Presentation | March 2026 | Versigent sits bottom left, and the Versigent logo sits bottom right.

**Visuals**

Three data tables, no charts or photographs. Table 1 (Adjusted Revenue Growth): header row dark grey #595959 with only the column heading 2025 in the right column; rows: Reported net sales % change 6%; Less: foreign currency exchange and commodities 2%; Adjusted revenue growth 4% (row in orange text with orange rule above and below). Table 2 (Free Cash Flow, $ millions): columns 2025, 2024, 2023, 2022; rows: Net cash provided by operating activities 641, 707, 180, 606; Impact of intercompany transfers of intellectual property and other related transaction (a) blank, blank, 284, blank; Less: capital expenditures (160), (206), (244), (223); Total Free Cash Flow 481, 501, 220, 383 (orange bold row). Footnote (a) below the table. Table 3 (Free Cash Flow Conversion, $ millions): columns 2025, 2024, 2023, 2022; rows: Free Cash Flow 481, 501, 220, 383; Net Income 528, 408, 423, 454; Free Cash Flow Conversion 91%, 123%, 52%, 84% (orange bold row). Highlighted points: total and conversion rows in orange #CD7925 with orange rules. Versigent logo is an orange rounded swirl mark followed by black lowercase wordmark.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with no decoration. Titles are black #000000 Calibri Bold sans-serif at about 3.7% of slide height. Table header bands #595959 with white bold Arial headings about 2.6% of slide height; body text black #000000 Arial regular about 2.2%; accent rows orange #CD7925 Arial bold. Alternating light grey #F5F5F5 row bands. Thin orange #CD7925 rules above and below accent rows. Footnote in Arial about 1.8%. Footer in Calibri about 2.0% grey #7F7F7F. No corner radii, shadows or gradients.

**Layout**

Two stacked full-width data tables sit left-aligned in one column with a left margin of about 3 percent and a right edge at about 97 percent of slide width. The title sits top-left at about 11 percent of slide height, above the tables. The first table (adjusted EBITDA reconciliation) spans about 21 to 66 percent of height, the second table (revenue and margin) about 74 to 84 percent, with a gap between them. Footer runs along the bottom at about 94 percent of height: page number at left, footer text next to it, Versigent logo at bottom right. No slide-level grid beyond these columns.

**Visuals**

Table 1: columns are a label column (left, about 3 to 62 percent of width, with blank spacer columns), then 2025, 2024, 2023, 2022 right-aligned value columns. Header row shows ($ millions) at left and the four years. Rows: Net income attributable to Versigent 528, 408, 423, 454; Interest (income) expense (3), (4), (4), (1); Income tax expense (6), 39, 144, 53; Net expense attributable to noncontrolling interest 18, 26, 29, (2); Depreciation and amortization 227, 202, 205, 191; EBITDA 764, 671, 797, 695 (highlighted orange bold); Other expense, net 10, 10, 8, 7; Equity income, net of tax (13), (22), (13), (20); Restructuring 86, 101, 48, 12; Separation costs 42, -, -, -; Other acquisition and portfolio project costs 4, 16, 12, 5; Russia/Ukraine Charges -, -, -, 54; Adjusted EBITDA 893, 776, 852, 753 (highlighted orange bold). Table 2: header ($ millions) 2025 2024 2023 2022; Revenue 8,818, 8,309, 8,832, 8,015; Adjusted EBITDA Margin 10.1%, 9.3%, 9.6%, 9.4% (orange bold). No chart. Versigent logo at bottom right: an orange abstract swoosh-like mark (two interlocking rounded leaf shapes) followed by the black wordmark Versigent in a lowercase sans-serif.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white slide with no gradients, shadows or imagery. Table header bars are dark grey #595959 with white bold Arial text. Body rows are light grey #F5F5F5 with thin light grey #BFBFBF horizontal separators. Key rows (EBITDA, Adjusted EBITDA, Adjusted EBITDA Margin) use orange #CD7925 bold text with orange rules: a red-orange hairline about #E0622B above the EBITDA row and orange #CD7925 rules above and below Adjusted EBITDA and below Adjusted EBITDA Margin. Title is Calibri Bold, black #000000, about 3.7 percent of slide height. Table body text is Arial regular, black #000000, about 2.2 percent of slide height. Footer is Calibri regular, grey #7F7F7F, about 2.0 percent of height. Rectangular cells, no corner radii.

Centered logo lockup on a full-bleed dark navy field; the logo sits horizontally across the vertical and horizontal centre with the orange emblem to the left of the white wordmark. Footer strip along the bottom-left with page number 32 followed by footer text, no title, no visible margin rules.

Logo: the Versigent mark is an orange (#CD7924) circular emblem made of three curved flame-like or leaf-like segments separated by two navy diagonal cuts, sitting to the left of the wordmark; the wordmark 'Versigent' is set in white geometric sans-serif lowercase except for the capital V, with a dot on the i and a descender on the g. No photographs, charts, or other graphics.

**Design**
